# Skills · T · part 135

846 entries, trajectory-inference-aucpr-evaluation to transcribe-clip. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **trajectory-inference-aucpr-evaluation**: Use when you have a sample-level distance matrix (e.g., Wasserstein distances between cluster distributions from .h5ad single-cell/pathomics cohorts) and need to infer a disease trajectory and score it by AUCPR and Spearman correlation against known severity labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-inference-aucpr-evaluation))
- **trajectory-inference-benchmarking**: Use when comparing single-cell trajectory/pseudo-time inference methods on scRNA-seq data with known capture or developmental stages, to quantify which tool's ordering explains real time via R-squared. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-inference-benchmarking))
- **trajectory-inference-method-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantitatively compare a new or existing trajectory-inference algorithm against baseline methods on synthetic single-cell data, replicating a published benchmark such as Supplementary Fig. 4g,h. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-inference-method-benchmarking--d726b1914700f6ab))
- **trajectory-inference-method-benchmarking**: Decision support for choosing a trajectory inference method when a branching single-cell dataset has trusted ground-truth population labels and lineage marker genes (e.g. Gata1, Pax5) to score branch assignments against. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-inference-method-benchmarking--7d3a5162b04ce8bc))
- **trajectory-inference-method-comparison**: Use when comparing dimension-reduction/trajectory methods on temporal scRNA-seq data where the goal is to quantify subpopulation separation within each time point, e.g. deciding whether TCM better preserves subpopulations than t-SNE or diffusion map. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-inference-method-comparison))
- **trajectory-inference-method-comparison-on-scrna-seq**: Decision support for selecting a TI method when a scRNA-seq dataset has a known or published branch/cell-type structure and the goal is to determine which of several TI methods (e.g., Monocle3 vs Mpath) reconstructs the true trajectory before downstream pseudotime analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-inference-method-comparison-on-scrna-seq))
- **trajectory-inference-method-selection**: Use when when you have an ArchR project object with processed single-cell ATAC-seq data and want to infer developmental or cell-state trajectories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/trajectory-inference-method-selection))
- **trajectory-inference-parameter-sweep**: Use when you need to assess how sensitive a clustering-based trajectory inference method (e.g. Totem) is to its clustering-count and k-range parameters, by running a grid of parameter combinations with multiple random seeds against a benchmark ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-inference-parameter-sweep))
- **trajectory-inference-preprocessing**: Use when you have raw or normalized single-cell RNA-seq expression data stored in an AnnData object (`.h5ad` format) and your analysis goal is to infer developmental or differentiation trajectories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/trajectory-inference-preprocessing))
- **trajectory-inference-pseudotime**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data has been processed in SingCellaR (Harmony-supervised embeddings plus Louvain clusters) and you need pseudotime ordering of immature-to-differentiated cells along a lineage such as hematopoietic stem/progenitor to erythroid. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-inference-pseudotime))
- **trajectory-inference-pseudotime-reconstruction**: Use when analyzing time-series scRNA-seq count matrices where cell asynchrony must be corrected via trajectory inference and pseudotime to identify genes and subclusters that vary over time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-inference-pseudotime-reconstruction))
- **trajectory-inference-terminal-state-detection**: Use when a bifurcating or multi-fate single-cell trajectory (scRNA-seq, scATAC-seq, integrated, or cytometry) must have its terminal fates and branch probabilities detected automatically without prior fate labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-inference-terminal-state-detection))
- **trajectory-inference-with-monocle3**: Use when a time-series scRNA-seq dataset (e.g. UMI counts from Fluidigm C1 or 10X) needs branch/pseudotime reconstruction before time-dependent covariate DEG or subcluster testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-inference-with-monocle3))
- **trajectory-inference-with-slingshot**: Use when paired lineage-barcode and scRNA-seq data need a cell-state tree (state_lineages) over k-means clusters of top-50 PCs, so a lineage-reconstruction method like LinRace can map mutations onto state transitions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-inference-with-slingshot))
- **trajectory-interpolation-alignment-between-lineages**: Use when you have two lineage-specific single-cell trajectories (e.g. Seurat objects with EPI/PE identities and a monocle3 cds) and need to test whether LR pairs co-vary and causally precede master TFs along pseudotime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-interpolation-alignment-between-lineages))
- **trajectory-knowledge**: Query the trajectory knowledge base for warm-start joint configurations and similar past trajectories. Use at the start of each step to seed Pinocchio IK, reducing convergence iterations from ~150 to ~20. Also stores successful trajectories after each completed batch for future runs. ([aws-samples/sample-industrial-multi-robot-agents](https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-industrial-multi-robot-agents/tree/HEAD/skills/trajectory-classic/trajectory-knowledge) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **trajectory-ledger**: Schema, grounding rules, outcome status and cost ledgers for agent trajectories. Use when an agent must record, replay or audit a multi-step run (task, tools, state transitions, cost, outcome) so that results are reproducible and auditable; not for logging application telemetry. - **trajectory-planning-eval**: 帮助研究者对多条候选轨迹或采样方案做结构化评估，给出维度对比、推荐排序与后续实验建议。 适用于任何科研场景下的轨迹/路径/方案筛选，包括分子动力学采样策略、合成路线选择、 实验方案序列对比等。当用户说「比较这几条轨迹」「评估哪个采样策略更好」「帮我选方案」 「这两个路线哪个更合理」「trajectory eval」时触发。即使用户没有明确说"轨迹"， 只要涉及 2 个及以上明确候选方案的结构化对比与筛选，且用户需要做出选择决策，也应触发本 skill。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/opentrajectory/trajectory-planning-eval) · ★16)
- **trajectory-prediction**: 動いているもの (タクシー、車、人、船) の軌跡の始まり (接頭) から、行き先を当てる。試験を「ある時刻に走っていたものを、その時刻で切る」作り方で作り、接頭の最後の点を基準にし、誤差を haversine の km で測り、区間は切った時刻ごとにまとめて出し、学習より後の時期でも基準との差が保たれるかを確かめる。行き先の塊の重み付き平均を出す MLP (ECML/PKDD 2015 の優勝モデル) を Porto で再現した手順。 ([yuiseki/GeoAI-master-skills](https://github.com/yuiseki/GeoAI-master-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/trajectory-prediction) · ★3)
- **trajectory-prediction**: Методология L4 (траектория мысли) + L6 (предсказание): каскадный лог гипотез, предсказание-верификация, 4D-гейт интрузивности. Детали грузятся по надобности. ([Nugnii/ClaudSoul](https://github.com/Nugnii/ClaudSoul/tree/HEAD/skills/trajectory-prediction))
